# 项目说明

## 项目定位

TradingAgents-AShare 是一个面向 A 股投资者的多智能体投研系统，核心理念：

- **模拟真实投研机构**：14 个专业角色分工协作
- **多空辩论机制**：多头研究员 vs 空头研究员，研究总监裁决
- **多模型厂商支持**：OpenAI / Anthropic / Google / 智谱 / DeepSeek / 小米等
- **意图驱动交互**：自然语言输入，自动识别标的和周期
- **持仓追踪**：导入持仓，自动跟踪，连续分析

## 当前产品事实（2026-07-10）

项目已从单一报告生成器扩展为分层投研工作台，但产品主线应保持收敛：

```text
Tree Work 既有研报/半年报知识页（历史上游）
  -> ZCode 知识库接入、研究评分与快照发布
  -> 来源与事实审计
  -> 昊天左侧候选
  -> TradeFlow 少量候选池
  -> 轻量 TA / 人工确认的完整 TA
  -> 观察仓、盘中观察、盘后复盘
```

TradeFlow 的 `TECH_TRADE` 与 `POLICY_AMBUSH/POLICY_CONFIRM` 必须分池展示，不能把短线技术机会当作中长期昊天候选。Fast Radar、Light Research、Full TA 的运行层级也必须保持清晰：完整 TA 只能人工确认触发。

现有 Tree Work 知识页只作为可追溯的历史上游与背景/观点证据；后续知识库接入、`research-scorer` 和正式快照发布统一由 ZCode 负责。公告、原始财报和行情数据仍是事实与动作门禁的优先来源。所有知识命中都必须带来源路径、报告期或更新时间、来源等级和新鲜度。

## 核心架构

### 1. 分析师团队（7 个）

| 角色 | 职责 | 数据源 |
|------|------|--------|
| 基本面分析师 | 财务指标、估值分析 | Tushare Pro（结构化主源）/AKShare/BaoStock |
| 情绪分析师 | 社交媒体情绪、舆情 | AKShare |
| 新闻分析师 | 新闻事件、政策影响 | AKShare |
| 技术分析师 | K线形态、技术指标 | AKShare/BaoStock |
| 宏观分析师 | 宏观经济、利率环境 | AKShare |
| 主力资金分析师 | 大单流向、主力动向 | Tushare Pro（个股资金主源）/AKShare |
| 量价分析师 | 成交量、量价关系 | AKShare/BaoStock |

### 2. 研究员团队

- **多头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看多 Claim
- **空头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看空 Claim
- **研究总监**：裁决多空辩论，形成投资计划

### 3. 风控团队

- **风控经理**：评估风险、设置止损止盈
- **合规检查**：确保建议符合投资规范

### 4. 交易员

- 根据研究计划和风控要求，输出最终交易建议

### 5. 编排层

- **LangGraph**：状态图编排，控制 Agent 间信息流转
- **辩论机制**：多轮辩论，每轮有 Claim → Counter-Claim → 裁决

## 数据流

```
用户输入（自然语言）
  ↓
意图识别 → 标的 + 周期
  ↓
数据获取（7 路并行）
  ↓
分析师团队（7 个并行分析）
  ↓
研究员辩论（多轮）
  ↓
风控评估
  ↓
交易建议输出
  ↓
研报存储 + 通知推送
```

## 模型配置

系统支持多层模型配置：

| 配置项 | 说明 | 默认值 |
|--------|------|--------|
| `deep_think_llm` | 深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `quick_think_llm` | 快速思考模型 | gpt-4o-mini |
| `mid_think_llm` | 中等思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `ultra_think_llm` | 超深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `backend_url` | API 端点 | https://api.openai.com/v1 |

支持通过环境变量或前端设置页切换模型厂商。

## API 接口

后端 FastAPI 提供以下主要接口：

- `POST /api/analyze` — 启动分析任务
- `GET /api/analysis/{id}` — 获取分析结果
- `GET /api/holdings` — 获取持仓
- `POST /api/watchlist` — 管理自选股
- `GET /api/reports` — 历史研报
- `POST /api/scheduler` — 定时任务管理

## 前端功能

- 意图驱动的自然语言交互
- 辩论对战可视化（多空对抗 Drawer）
- 持仓追踪与跟踪看板
- 结构化研报管理
- 定时分析配置
- 模型厂商设置

--- DECISIONS TAIL ---
# 架构决策记录

> 记录项目中的关键架构决策，方便后续回溯"为什么这样做"。

---

## ADR-001: 采用 LangGraph 编排多智能体
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要 14 个 Agent 按特定流程协作，有循环（辩论）和条件分支
- **决策**：使用 LangGraph 状态图编排，而非简单的链式调用
- **原因**：LangGraph 原生支持循环、条件路由、状态管理，适合辩论机制
- **影响**：所有 Agent 间通信通过 Graph State 传递，新增 Agent 需要注册到图中

## ADR-002: 多模型厂商适配层
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：不同 Agent 对模型能力需求不同，用户可能有不同的模型偏好
- **决策**：通过 `llm_clients/` 统一适配，支持通过配置切换厂商和模型
- **原因**：避免硬编码单一厂商，降低用户使用门槛
- **影响**：新增模型厂商需要在 `llm_clients/` 中添加适配器

## ADR-003: SQLite 作为持久化方案
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要存储研报、自选股、定时任务等结构化数据
- **决策**：使用 SQLite，单文件部署，无需额外数据库服务
- **原因**：本地部署场景，轻量级，零运维
- **影响**：并发写入有限，定时任务和分析任务需要串行化写入

## ADR-004: OpenClaw 作为项目协作调度层
- **日期**：2026-05-09
- **背景**：需要 OpenClaw、OpenCode、OpenCode TUI 三者协作处理同一项目
- **决策**：OpenClaw 负责任务判断和调度，不直接做代码修改；OpenCode 负责代码执行；OpenCode TUI 负责审查
- **原因**：OpenClaw 有完整的 agent 调度能力（exec / sessions_spawn / cron），适合做编排层
- **影响**：所有代码修改任务必须经过 OpenClaw 判断复杂度后决定执行路径

## ADR-005: Git 仓库 + docs/ 作为项目事实源
- **日期**：2026-05-09
- **背景**：多个工具需要读取同一套项目上下文
- **决策**：不使用任何 agent 的独立记忆系统保存项目状态，所有关键信息写入 Git 仓库的 docs/ 目录
- **原因**：避免信息分散、版本冲突、单点失效
- **影响**：每次任务开始前必须读取 AGENTS.md + docs/ 下的上下文文件

## ADR-006: 2026-07-10 将研报/半年报/昊天闭环设为唯一产品主线
- **日期**：2026-07-10
- **背景**：系统已有 TradeFlow、跟踪看板、知识库和自动开发等大量工程能力，但真实候选中仍以技术池为主，观察仓和盘后 Review 的生产记录不足，半年报事实 Provider 尚未完成。
- **决策**：优先完成 HY-002/HY-003/KB-015 及其后续事实审计，再做昊天真实候选和交易日回放；PLAYBOOK-002~006 暂停，除非直接阻塞主线。
- **原因**：用户核心目标是从政策、产业和研报中找到少量高质量中长期候选，而不是继续扩大功能数量。先验证选股和证据闭环，才能判断后续仓位规则是否值得实现。
- **影响**：自动领取顺序以 `docs/TASKS.md` 的“当前执行队列”为准；fixture 验收和真实运行验收必须分开记录；新功能必须说明是否直接服务主线。

## ADR-007: 研究评分采用四卡分层所有权，不生成混合总分
- **日期**：2026-07-13
- **背景**：现有 Tree Work 知识库、TA readiness、TradeFlow 候选分、账户风控和 PLAYBOOK 已分别存在评分或阶段字段。若各层都计算完整总分和动作，会产生重复计分、阈值冲突，以及“好公司被误判为现在可以买”的风险。
- **决策**：`research-scorer` 的代码、规则版本和正式快照写入归 ZCode 知识库接入项目所有；ZCode 发布的知识快照只拥有慢变量 `research_evidence_confidence` 与 `thesis_quality`。现有 Tree Work 页面继续作为上游知识输入，不要求历史改名。TradingAgents-AShare 不复刻评分公式，只读取版本化快照；TradeFlow 复用现有动态因子生成 `entry_timing`，账户上下文生成 `portfolio_fit`，TA 在四卡之后统一执行硬性否决并映射 PLAYBOOK 七阶段。禁止合成单一股票总分，禁止知识快照携带或覆盖交易动作。
- **原因**：证据可信度、公司逻辑、实时买点和账户资格回答的是四个不同问题；分层后才能做到来源可追溯、动态变化可解释，并让高研究分始终受行情、账户和风控约束。
- **影响**：知识库侧任务单独记录在 `docs/zcode_research_scorer_handoff.md`；本仓库新增 SCORE-001~006 消费与裁决任务线。`execution_action` 与 Buy/Risk Level 继续作为底层安全契约，`playbook_stage` 只提供更细语义。SCORE-005 可以实现不含仓位数量的阶段判定，但不解锁 PLAYBOOK-002/003 的真实三笔法和仓位拆分规则。SCORE-006 必须等真实 ZCode 快照人工确认后释放，fixture 不得冒充跨项目验收。

---

```
- **日期**：YYYY-MM-DD

- **决策**：[做了什么决定]
- **原因**：[为什么选这个方案]
- **影响**：[对项目的影响]
```

+  - **P1 启用回调即抛异常**（`scheduler/main.py` `_load_report_payload`）：

+# （系统追加诊断块的起始位置）持久化到 result_data.metadata。解析必须优先限定
+# 在该可信尾部；模型正文提及的 [C-008] 完整度/置信度不得发布为 available。
+_C008_MARKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\[C-008\]")
+_READINESS_COMPLETENESS_PATTERN = re.compile(r"数据完整度[：:]\s*(\d{1,3})\s*%")
+_READINESS_CONFIDENCE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"置信度[：:]\s*([高中低])")
+
+
+def _extract_system_diagnostics_offset(*sources: Any) -> Any:
+    """Return the persisted ``metadata.system_diagnostics_offset`` if present.
+
+    Scans dict-like sources (``result_data`` / ``report_obj["result_data"]``)
+    for ``metadata.system_diagnostics_offset``. Returns None when no source
+    carries the key; the raw (possibly invalid) value otherwise so the parser
+    can distinguish "absent" (legacy report) from "present but inconsistent".
+    """
+    for source in sources:
+        if not isinstance(source, dict):
+            continue
+        metadata = source.get("metadata")
+        if isinstance(metadata, dict) and "system_diagnostics_offset" in metadata:
+            return metadata["system_diagnostics_offset"]
+    return None
+
+
+def _parse_readiness_from_text(text: Any, trusted_offset: Any = None) -> dict | None:
+    """Parse the authoritative persisted [C-008] readiness block from report text.
+
+    ``trusted_offset`` is the persisted ``metadata.system_diagnostics_offset``:
+
+    - valid int within ``[0, len(text))`` — parse only that system-appended
+      tail; a ``[C-008]`` mention authored by the model before the offset is
+      never trusted, even when it carries completeness/confidence values;
+    - present but invalid (non-int / out of range) — the persisted text is
+      inconsistent with the offset, so nothing is trusted;
+    - absent (legacy reports without the metadata) — fall back to the last
+      ``[C-008]`` heading, because the system block is appended after any
+      model-authored text.
+
+    Returns ``{"data_completeness": int, "confidence": str}`` (either key may
+    be present alone) or None when the block or its values are absent/invalid.
+    """
+    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
+        return None
+    source = text
+    if trusted_offset is not None:
+        if isinstance(trusted_offset, bool) or not isinstance(trusted_offset, int):
+            return None
+        if not 0 <= trusted_offset < len(text):
+            return None
+        source = text[trusted_offset:]
+    marker = None
+    for marker in _C008_MARKER_PATTERN.finditer(source):
+        pass  # keep the last occurrence = system-appended block
+    if marker is None:
+        return None
+    section = source[marker.end():]
+    parsed: dict[str, Any] = {}
+    completeness = _READINESS_COMPLETENESS_PATTERN.search(section)
+    if completeness:
+        value = int(completeness.group(1))
+        if 0 <= value <= 100:
+            parsed["data_completeness"] = value
+    confidence = _READINESS_CONFIDENCE_PATTERN.search(section)
+    if confidence:
+        parsed["confidence"] = confidence.group(1)
+    return parsed or None
+
+

     url = os.getenv("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_URL", "").strip()

+            "source": readiness_source,
+        }
+    else:
+        readiness_score = {"status": "not_available"}

+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# [B-002-R2-fix] Test: readiness parsing restricted to the trusted system tail

+# Model-authored body mentions [C-008] with fabricated values BEFORE the
+# system-appended diagnostics (production order in risk_manager).
+_MODEL_BODY_FAKE_C008 = (
+    "模型正文引用 [C-008] 并声称数据完整度：12%，置信度：低。\n"
+    "该引用并非系统评分块。"
+)

+_SYSTEM_TAIL = (
+    "📋 [C-008] 执行就绪度评分\n"
+    "- 数据完整度：87%\n"
+    "- 置信度：高\n"
+    "- 报告等级：高质量报告 · 已持仓\n"
+    "- 总结：数据完整，置信度高。"
+)
+
+
+def _persisted_decision_text() -> tuple[str, int]:
+    """Production-shaped text: model body + system tail, with persisted offset."""
+    text = _MODEL_BODY_FAKE_C008 + "\n\n" + _SYSTEM_TAIL
+    offset = len(_MODEL_BODY_FAKE_C008) + 2  # risk_manager: len(visible_body) + 2
+    assert text[offset:].startswith("📋 [C-008]")
+    return text, offset
+

+
+        text, offset = _persisted_decision_text()
+        assert _parse_readiness_from_text(text, trusted_offset=offset) == {
+            "data_completeness": 87,
+            "confidence": "高",
+        }
+        # Trusted window without any [C-008] marker → None
+        no_marker_text = "正文没有任何标记。\n\n📊 数据源可用性"
+        assert (
+            _parse_readiness_from_text(
+                no_marker_text, trusted_offset=len("正文没有任何标记。\n\n")
+            )
+            is None
+        )
+        # No offset info → last-heading fallback
+        assert _parse_readiness_from_text(text) == {
+            "data_completeness": 87,
+            "confidence": "高",
+        }
+
+

     1	# [B-002] openclaw_callback
     2	"""OpenClaw 定时任务完成回调 service.
     3	
     4	定时分析完成后，构建结构化 payload 并 POST 到 OpenClaw 回调端点，
     5	由主控 AI 决定是否推送到飞书 / 企业微信等下游通道。
     6	
     7	设计契约：
     8	- ``OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_URL``  环境变量：OpenClaw 接收 webhook 的地址。
     9	- ``OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_ENABLED`` 环境变量：是否启用回调（默认 false）。
    10	- ``readiness_score`` 恒出现在 payload 中（v1.1.0 起）：
    11	  - 有真实来源时 ``{"status": "available", "data_completeness": int,
    12	    "confidence": "高|中|低", "source": "readiness_score|final_trade_decision"}``；
    13	  - 无来源时 ``{"status": "not_available"}``，不以空值或缺键冒充。
    14	- 不读取 / 打印任何 API key / token（与 notification_draft_service 一致）。
    15	- 失败时静默降级，不阻塞主流程。
    16	- 不写数据库、不调 LLM、不改 prompts。
    17	"""
    18	
    19	from __future__ import annotations
    20	
    21	import asyncio
    22	import json
    23	import logging
    24	import os
    25	import re
    26	import time
    27	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    28	from typing import Any, Optional
    29	
    30	import requests
    31	
    32	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    33	
    34	CALLBACK_SCHEMA_VERSION = "1.1.0"
    35	CALLBACK_SOURCE = "tradingagents-scheduler"
    36	
    37	# Retry config
    38	_MAX_RETRIES = 2
    39	_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS = 10
    40	_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 15

    42	
    43	def _pick(*values):
    44	    """Return first non-null value, allowing explicit empty-string fallback."""
    45	    for value in values:
    46	        if value is None:
    47	            continue
    48	        return value
    49	    return None
    50	
    51	
    52	def _extract(source: Any, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
    53	    """Extract field from dict-like/ORM-like source."""
    54	    if source is None:
    55	        return default
    56	    if isinstance(source, dict):
    57	        return source.get(key, default)
    58	    if hasattr(source, key):
    59	        return getattr(source, key, default)
    60	    return default
    61	
    62	
    63	def _env_bool(name: str, default: bool) -> bool:
    64	    value = os.getenv(name)
    65	    if value is None:
    66	        return default
    67	    return value.strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
    68	
    69	
    70	def _clip_text(text: str | None, limit: int = 500) -> str:
    71	    if not text:
    72	        return ""
    73	    compact = " ".join(str(text).split()).strip()
    74	    return compact[:limit]
    75	
    76	
    77	# [B-002-R2] readiness 真源：risk_manager 把 [C-008] 执行就绪度评分块追加到
    78	# final_trade_decision 文本并随 ReportDB 持久化；结构化 dict 无生产写入方，
    79	# 仅作为前向兼容输入。文本块是当前唯一真实持久化来源。
    80	# [B-002-R2-fix] 权威块定位：risk_manager 同时把 metadata.system_diagnostics_offset
    81	# （系统追加诊断块的起始位置）持久化到 result_data.metadata。解析必须优先限定
    82	# 在该可信尾部；模型正文提及的 [C-008] 完整度/置信度不得发布为 available。
    83	_C008_MARKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\[C-008\]")
    84	_READINESS_COMPLETENESS_PATTERN = re.compile(r"数据完整度[：:]\s*(\d{1,3})\s*%")
    85	_READINESS_CONFIDENCE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"置信度[：:]\s*([高中低])")
    86	
    87	
    88	def _extract_system_diagnostics_offset(*sources: Any) -> Any:
    89	    """Return the persisted ``metadata.system_diagnostics_offset`` if present.
    90	
    91	    Scans dict-like sources (``result_data`` / ``report_obj["result_data"]``)
    92	    for ``metadata.system_diagnostics_offset``. Returns None when no source
    93	    carries the key; the raw (possibly invalid) value otherwise so the parser
    94	    can distinguish "absent" (legacy report) from "present but inconsistent".
    95	    """
    96	    for source in sources:
    97	        if not isinstance(source, dict):
    98	            continue
    99	        metadata = source.get("metadata")
   100	        if isinstance(metadata, dict) and "system_diagnostics_offset" in metadata:
   101	            return metadata["system_diagnostics_offset"]
   102	    return None
   103	
   104	
   105	def _parse_readiness_from_text(text: Any, trusted_offset: Any = None) -> dict | None:
   106	    """Parse the authoritative persisted [C-008] readiness block from report text.
   107	
   108	    ``trusted_offset`` is the persisted ``metadata.system_diagnostics_offset``:
   109	
   110	    - valid int within ``[0, len(text))`` — parse only that system-appended
   111	      tail; a ``[C-008]`` mention authored by the model before the offset is
   112	      never trusted, even when it carries completeness/confidence values;
   113	    - present but invalid (non-int / out of range) — the persisted text is
   114	      inconsistent with the offset, so nothing is trusted;
   115	    - absent (legacy reports without the metadata) — fall back to the last
   116	      ``[C-008]`` heading, because the system block is appended after any
   117	      model-authored text.
   118	
   119	    Returns ``{"data_completeness": int, "confidence": str}`` (either key may
   120	    be present alone) or None when the block or its values are absent/invalid.
   121	    """
   122	    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
   123	        return None
   124	    source = text
   125	    if trusted_offset is not None:
   126	        if isinstance(trusted_offset, bool) or not isinstance(trusted_offset, int):
   127	            return None
   128	        if not 0 <= trusted_offset < len(text):
   129	            return None
   130	        source = text[trusted_offset:]
   131	    marker = None
   132	    for marker in _C008_MARKER_PATTERN.finditer(source):
   133	        pass  # keep the last occurrence = system-appended block
   134	    if marker is None:
   135	        return None
   136	    section = source[marker.end():]
   137	    parsed: dict[str, Any] = {}
   138	    completeness = _READINESS_COMPLETENESS_PATTERN.search(section)
   139	    if completeness:
   140	        value = int(completeness.group(1))
   141	        if 0 <= value <= 100:
   142	            parsed["data_completeness"] = value
   143	    confidence = _READINESS_CONFIDENCE_PATTERN.search(section)
   144	    if confidence:
   145	        parsed["confidence"] = confidence.group(1)
   146	    return parsed or None
   147	
   148	
   149	def is_openclaw_callback_enabled() -> bool:
   150	    """Check if OpenClaw callback is enabled and URL is configured."""
   151	    url = os.getenv("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_URL", "").strip()
   152	    enabled = _env_bool("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_ENABLED", False)
   153	    return enabled and bool(url)
   154	
   155	
   156	def build_callback_payload(
   157	    report_id: str,
   158	    symbol: str,
   159	    trade_date: str,
   160	    *,
   161	    report_obj: dict | None = None,
   162	    user_id: str = "",
   163	    horizon: str = "short",
   164	    source: str = "scheduled",
   165	    result_data: dict | None = None,
   166	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   167	    """Build structured callback payload from a completed report.
   168	
   169	    Extracts decision/direction/action/confidence/summary from result_data
   170	    into a compact dict for OpenClaw consumption.
   171	
   172	    Args:
   173	        report_id: Report/job ID.
   174	        symbol: Stock symbol.
   175	        trade_date: Trade date string (YYYY-MM-DD).
   176	        user_id: User who owns the scheduled task.
   177	        horizon: Analysis horizon (short/medium).
   178	        source: How the analysis was triggered (scheduled/scheduled_manual).
   179	        result_data: Full report result_data dict (from ReportDB.result_data).
   180	
   181	    Returns:
   182	        Structured callback payload dict.
   183	    """
   184	    report_data = report_obj or {}
   185	    result = result_data or {}
   186	
   187	    # Extract semantic fields from result_data (same priority as wecom service)
   188	    action = _pick(
   189	        _extract(report_data, "action_label"),
   190	        _extract(result, "action_label"),
   191	        _extract(report_data, "decision"),
   192	        _extract(result, "decision"),
   193	        "",
   194	    )
   195	    direction = _pick(
   196	        _extract(report_data, "research_direction"),
   197	        _extract(result, "research_direction"),
   198	        _extract(report_data, "direction"),
   199	        _extract(result, "direction"),
   200	        "",
   201	    )
   202	    execution_action = _pick(
   203	        _extract(report_data, "execution_action"),
   204	        _extract(result, "execution_action"),
   205	        "",
   206	    )
   207	    confidence = _pick(_extract(report_data, "confidence"), _extract(result, "confidence"))
   208	
   209	    # Build summary from available text fields
   210	    summary = _clip_text(
   211	        _pick(
   212	            _extract(report_data, "final_trade_decision"),
   213	            _extract(result, "final_trade_decision"),
   214	            _extract(report_data, "trader_investment_plan"),
   215	            _extract(result, "trader_investment_plan"),
   216	            _extract(report_data, "investment_plan"),
   217	            _extract(result, "investment_plan"),
   218	            "",
   219	        )
   220	    )
   221	
   222	    risk_items = _pick(
   223	        _extract(report_data, "risk_items"),
   224	        _extract(result, "risk_items"),
   225	        [],
   226	    )
   227	    key_metrics = _pick(
   228	        _extract(report_data, "key_metrics"),
   229	        _extract(result, "key_metrics"),
   230	        [],
   231	    )
   232	
   233	    risk_summary = []
   234	    for item in risk_items[:5]:  # Cap at 5
   235	        if isinstance(item, dict):
   236	            risk_summary.append({
   237	                "name": item.get("name", ""),
   238	                "level": item.get("level", "unknown"),
   239	            })
   240	
   241	    metrics_summary = []
   242	    for metric in key_metrics[:8]:  # Cap at 8
   243	        if isinstance(metric, dict):
   244	            metrics_summary.append({
   245	                "name": metric.get("name", ""),
   246	                "value": metric.get("value", ""),
   247	                "status": metric.get("status", "unknown"),
   248	            })
   249	
   250	    # [B-002-R2] readiness_score：优先结构化字段（前向兼容），否则从已持久化
   251	    # 的 [C-008] 文本块解析；两者皆无时输出明确 not_available 状态。
   252	    readiness_raw = _pick(
   253	        _extract(report_data, "readiness_score"),
   254	        _extract(result, "readiness_score"),
   255	    )
   256	    readiness_values: dict | None = None
   257	    readiness_source: str | None = None
   258	    if isinstance(readiness_raw, dict):
   259	        dc = readiness_raw.get("data_completeness")
   260	        conf = readiness_raw.get("confidence")
   261	        if dc is not None or conf is not None:
   262	            readiness_values = {"data_completeness": dc, "confidence": conf}
   263	            readiness_source = "readiness_score"
   264	    if readiness_values is None:
   265	        # [B-002-R2-fix] restrict text parsing to the trusted system tail
   266	        # persisted in metadata.system_diagnostics_offset (report_service and
   267	        # the frontend already consume the same field).
   268	        trusted_offset = _extract_system_diagnostics_offset(
   269	            _extract(report_data, "result_data"),
   270	            result,
   271	        )
   272	        for text in (
   273	            _extract(report_data, "final_trade_decision"),
   274	            _extract(result, "final_trade_decision"),
   275	        ):
   276	            parsed = _parse_readiness_from_text(text, trusted_offset=trusted_offset)
   277	            if parsed:
   278	                readiness_values = parsed
   279	                readiness_source = "final_trade_decision"
   280	                break
   281	    if readiness_values is not None:
   282	        readiness_score: dict[str, Any] = {
   283	            "status": "available",
   284	            "data_completeness": readiness_values.get("data_completeness"),
   285	            "confidence": readiness_values.get("confidence"),
   286	            "source": readiness_source,
   287	        }
   288	    else:
   289	        readiness_score = {"status": "not_available"}
   290	
   291	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   292	
   293	    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   294	        "schema_version": CALLBACK_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   295	        "source": CALLBACK_SOURCE,
   296	        "event": "scheduled_analysis_completed",
   297	        "timestamp": now.isoformat(),
   298	        "report": {
   299	            "id": report_id,
   300	            "symbol": symbol,
   301	            "trade_date": trade_date,
   302	            "horizon": horizon,
   303	            "source": source,
   304	        },
   305	        "decision": {
   306	            "action": action,
   307	            "direction": direction,
   308	            "execution_action": execution_action,
   309	            "confidence": confidence,
   310	        },
   311	        "summary": summary,
   312	        "risk_items": risk_summary,
   313	        "key_metrics": metrics_summary,
   314	    }
   315	
   316	    payload["readiness_score"] = readiness_score
   317	
   318	    if user_id:
   319	        payload["user_id"] = user_id
   320	
   321	    return payload
   322	
   323	
   324	def send_callback(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   325	    """POST callback payload to OpenClaw webhook URL.
   326	
   327	    Args:
   328	        payload: Structured callback payload.
   329	
   330	    Returns:
   331	        True if sent successfully, False otherwise.
   332	    """
   333	    url = os.getenv("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_URL", "").strip()
   334	    if not url:
   335	        logger.debug("[B-002] OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_URL not configured, skipping")
   336	        return False
   337	
   338	    headers = {
   339	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   340	        "User-Agent": "TradingAgents-Scheduler/1.0",
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    # Optional auth header
   344	    auth_token = os.getenv("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_TOKEN", "").strip()
   345	    if auth_token:
   346	        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {auth_token}"
   347	
   348	    body = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
   349	
   350	    for attempt in range(_MAX_RETRIES + 1):
   351	        try:
   352	            response = requests.post(
   353	                url,
   354	                data=body.encode("utf-8"),
   355	                headers=headers,
   356	                timeout=_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   357	            )
   358	            if 200 <= response.status_code < 300:
   359	                logger.info(
   360	                    "[B-002] OpenClaw callback sent OK (status=%d, symbol=%s)",
   361	                    response.status_code,
   362	                    payload.get("report", {}).get("symbol", "?"),
   363	                )
   364	                return True
   365	            logger.warning(
   366	                "[B-002] OpenClaw callback HTTP %d (attempt %d/%d): %s",
   367	                response.status_code,
   368	                attempt + 1,
   369	                _MAX_RETRIES + 1,
   370	                _clip_text(getattr(response, "text", None), 200),
   371	            )
   372	        except requests.RequestException as exc:
   373	            logger.warning(
   374	                "[B-002] OpenClaw callback failed (attempt %d/%d): %s",
   375	                attempt + 1,
   376	                _MAX_RETRIES + 1,
   377	                exc,
   378	            )
   379	
   380	        if attempt < _MAX_RETRIES:
   381	            time.sleep(_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS)
   382	
   383	    logger.error(
   384	        "[B-002] OpenClaw callback exhausted retries for symbol=%s",
   385	        payload.get("report", {}).get("symbol", "?"),
   386	    )
   387	    return False
   388	
   389	
   390	async def send_callback_async(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   391	    """Async wrapper around send_callback."""
   392	    return await asyncio.to_thread(send_callback, payload)
   393

   395	def notify_openclaw_on_report_completion(
   396	    report_id: str,
   397	    symbol: str,
   398	    trade_date: str,
   399	    *,
   400	    report_obj: dict | None = None,
   401	    user_id: str = "",
   402	    horizon: str = "short",
   403	    source: str = "scheduled",
   404	    result_data: dict | None = None,
   405	) -> None:
   406	    """Fire-and-forget OpenClaw notification for a completed scheduled report.
   407	
   408	    Called from the scheduler after a scheduled analysis completes.
   409	    Builds the payload and sends it asynchronously (non-blocking).
   410	
   411	    Args:

   413	        symbol: Stock symbol.
   414	        trade_date: Trade date string.
   415	        user_id: User who owns the scheduled task.
   416	        horizon: Analysis horizon.
   417	        source: How the analysis was triggered.
   418	        report_obj: Full report object as fallback/overrides for key fields.
   419	        result_data: Full report result_data dict.
   420	    """
   421	    if not is_openclaw_callback_enabled():
   422	        return
   423	
   424	    payload = build_callback_payload(
   425	        report_id=report_id,
   426	        symbol=symbol,
   427	        trade_date=trade_date,
   428	        user_id=user_id,
   429	        horizon=horizon,
   430	        source=source,

   192	                        email_user = user
   193	            return email_user, report_to_send, webhook_url, wecom_report_enabled
   194

   214	async def _send_openclaw_callback(
   215	    user_id: str, report_id: str, symbol: str, trade_date: str, horizon: str, source: str
   216	) -> None:
   217	    """Send OpenClaw callback after scheduled analysis completion.
   218	
   219	    [B-002] Loads report result_data and sends a structured payload to the
   220	    configured OpenClaw callback URL.  OpenClaw (主控 AI) decides whether
   221	    to push to 飞书 / 企业微信.
   222	    """
   223	    try:
   224	        from api.services.openclaw_callback_service import (
   225	            is_openclaw_callback_enabled,
   226	            notify_openclaw_on_report_completion,
   227	        )
   228	
   229	        if not is_openclaw_callback_enabled():
   230	            return
   231	
   232	        def _load_report_payload() -> dict | None:
   233	            with get_db_ctx() as db:
   234	                report = db.query(ReportDB).filter(ReportDB.id == report_id).first()
   235	                if report:
   236	                    if not isinstance(report.result_data, dict):
   237	                        report.result_data = {}
   238	                    # [B-002-R2] only fields that exist on ReportDB schema;
   239	                    # horizon/analysis_summary/opinion are not ORM columns and
   240	                    # raised AttributeError on read. horizon comes from the
   241	                    # task argument; readiness is parsed by the callback
   242	                    # service from the persisted final_trade_decision block.
   243	                    return {
   244	                        "id": report.id,
   245	                        "symbol": report.symbol,
   246	                        "trade_date": report.trade_date,
   247	                        "result_data": report.result_data,
   248	                        "decision": report.decision,
   249	                        "direction": report.direction,
   250	                        "research_direction": report.research_direction,
   251	                        "execution_action": report.execution_action,
   252	                        "action_label": report.action_label,
   253	                        "confidence": report.confidence,
   254	                        "risk_items": report.risk_items,
   255	                        "key_metrics": report.key_metrics,
   256	                        "final_trade_decision": report.final_trade_decision,
   257	                        "trader_investment_plan": report.trader_investment_plan,
   258	                        "investment_plan": report.investment_plan,
   259	                    }
   260	                return None
   261	
   262	        report_obj = await asyncio.to_thread(_load_report_payload)
   263	        result_data = report_obj.get("result_data") if isinstance(report_obj, dict) else None
   264	        notify_openclaw_on_report_completion(
   265	            report_id=report_id,
   266	            symbol=symbol,
   267	            trade_date=trade_date,
   268	            user_id=user_id,
   269	            horizon=horizon,
   270	            source=source,
   271	            report_obj=report_obj if isinstance(report_obj, dict) else None,
   272	            result_data=result_data,
   273	        )
   274	        _log(f"[Scheduler] OpenClaw callback queued for {symbol}")
   275	    except Exception as e:
   276	        logger.warning(f"[Scheduler] OpenClaw callback failed for {symbol}: {e}")

   279	# [M-010] feishu_notification_confirmation
   280	async def _send_feishu_notification_draft(
   281	    user_id: str, report_id: str, symbol: str
   282	) -> None:
   283	    """Auto-generate pending feishu notification drafts after scheduled analysis.
   284	
   285	    Unlike email/WeCom which send immediately, feishu notifications are stored
   286	    as 'pending_confirmation' for human review before sending.
   287	    """
   288	    try:
   289	        from api.services.feishu_webhook_service import is_feishu_webhook_enabled
   290	        from api.services.notification_confirmation_service import generate_pending
   291	
   292	        if not is_feishu_webhook_enabled():
   293	            return
   294	
   295	        def _generate():
   296	            with get_db_ctx() as db:
   297	                return generate_pending(db, user_id, channel="feishu")
   298	
   299	        result = await asyncio.to_thread(_generate)
   300	        generated = result.get("generated_count", 0)
   301	        if generated > 0:
   302	            _log(f"[Scheduler] Generated {generated} feishu notification draft(s) for {symbol}")
   303	    except Exception as e:
   304	        logger.warning(f"[Scheduler] Feishu notification draft failed for {symbol}: {e}")

--- callback callsite ---
   215	    user_id: str, report_id: str, symbol: str, trade_date: str, horizon: str, source: str
   216	) -> None:
   217	    """Send OpenClaw callback after scheduled analysis completion.
   218	
   219	    [B-002] Loads report result_data and sends a structured payload to the
   220	    configured OpenClaw callback URL.  OpenClaw (主控 AI) decides whether
   221	    to push to 飞书 / 企业微信.
   222	    """
   223	    try:
   224	        from api.services.openclaw_callback_service import (
   225	            is_openclaw_callback_enabled,
   226	            notify_openclaw_on_report_completion,
   227	        )
   228	
   229	        if not is_openclaw_callback_enabled():
   230	            return
   231	
   232	        def _load_report_payload() -> dict | None:
   233	            with get_db_ctx() as db:
   234	                report = db.query(ReportDB).filter(ReportDB.id == report_id).first()
   235	                if report:
   236	                    if not isinstance(report.result_data, dict):
   237	                        report.result_data = {}
   238	                    # [B-002-R2] only fields that exist on ReportDB schema;
   239	                    # horizon/analysis_summary/opinion are not ORM columns and
   240	                    # raised AttributeError on read. horizon comes from the
   241	                    # task argument; readiness is parsed by the callback
   242	                    # service from the persisted final_trade_decision block.
   243	                    return {
   244	                        "id": report.id,
   245	                        "symbol": report.symbol,
   246	                        "trade_date": report.trade_date,
   247	                        "result_data": report.result_data,
   248	                        "decision": report.decision,
   249	                        "direction": report.direction,
   250	                        "research_direction": report.research_direction,
   251	                        "execution_action": report.execution_action,
   252	                        "action_label": report.action_label,
   253	                        "confidence": report.confidence,
   254	                        "risk_items": report.risk_items,
   255	                        "key_metrics": report.key_metrics,
   256	                        "final_trade_decision": report.final_trade_decision,
   257	                        "trader_investment_plan": report.trader_investment_plan,
   258	                        "investment_plan": report.investment_plan,
   259	                    }
   260	                return None
   261	
   262	        report_obj = await asyncio.to_thread(_load_report_payload)
   263	        result_data = report_obj.get("result_data") if isinstance(report_obj, dict) else None
   264	        notify_openclaw_on_report_completion(
   265	            report_id=report_id,
   266	            symbol=symbol,
   267	            trade_date=trade_date,
   268	            user_id=user_id,
   269	            horizon=horizon,
   270	            source=source,
   271	            report_obj=report_obj if isinstance(report_obj, dict) else None,
   272	            result_data=result_data,
   273	        )
   274	        _log(f"[Scheduler] OpenClaw callback queued for {symbol}")
   275	    except Exception as e:
   276	        logger.warning(f"[Scheduler] OpenClaw callback failed for {symbol}: {e}")

--- notify tail ---
   390	async def send_callback_async(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   391	    """Async wrapper around send_callback."""
   392	    return await asyncio.to_thread(send_callback, payload)
   393	
   394	
   395	def notify_openclaw_on_report_completion(
   396	    report_id: str,
   397	    symbol: str,
   398	    trade_date: str,
   399	    *,
   400	    report_obj: dict | None = None,
   401	    user_id: str = "",
   402	    horizon: str = "short",
   403	    source: str = "scheduled",
   404	    result_data: dict | None = None,
   405	) -> None:
   406	    """Fire-and-forget OpenClaw notification for a completed scheduled report.
   407	
   408	    Called from the scheduler after a scheduled analysis completes.
   409	    Builds the payload and sends it asynchronously (non-blocking).
   410	
   411	    Args:
   412	        report_id: Report/job ID.
   413	        symbol: Stock symbol.
   414	        trade_date: Trade date string.
   415	        user_id: User who owns the scheduled task.
   416	        horizon: Analysis horizon.
   417	        source: How the analysis was triggered.
   418	        report_obj: Full report object as fallback/overrides for key fields.
   419	        result_data: Full report result_data dict.
   420	    """
   421	    if not is_openclaw_callback_enabled():
   422	        return
   423	
   424	    payload = build_callback_payload(
   425	        report_id=report_id,
   426	        symbol=symbol,
   427	        trade_date=trade_date,
   428	        user_id=user_id,
   429	        horizon=horizon,
   430	        source=source,
   431	        report_obj=report_obj,
   432	        result_data=result_data,
   433	    )
   434	
   435	    # Fire-and-forget: caller already in async context, schedule as background task
   436	    try:
   437	        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   438	        loop.create_task(send_callback_async(payload))
   439	    except RuntimeError:
   440	        # No running loop (e.g. sync context) — try sync send in background thread
   441	        import threading
   442	        thread = threading.Thread(
   443	            target=send_callback,
   444	            args=(payload,),
   445	            daemon=True,
   446	            name=f"openclaw-callback-{symbol}",
   447	        )
   448	        thread.start()
   449	
   450	
   451	__all__ = [
   452	    "build_callback_payload",
   453	    "send_callback",
   454	    "send_callback_async",
   455	    "is_openclaw_callback_enabled",
   456	    "notify_openclaw_on_report_completion",
   457	    "CALLBACK_SCHEMA_VERSION",
   458	    "CALLBACK_SOURCE",
   459	]

  1161	    match = re.search(r"<!--\s*VERDICT:\s*(\{.*?\})\s*-->", text, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
  1162	    if not match:
  1163	        return None
  1164	    try:
  1165	        # Clean potential newlines or invisible characters common in LLM outputs
  1166	        raw_json = match.group(1).strip().replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ')
  1167	        payload = json.loads(raw_json)
  1168	    except Exception:
  1169	        return None
  1170	    direction = str(payload.get("direction") or "").strip()
  1171	    reason = str(payload.get("reason") or "").strip()
  1172	    if not direction:
  1173	        return None
  1174	    return {"direction": direction, "reason": reason}

  1177	def resolve_report_fields(
  1178	    result_data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
  1179	    confidence_override: Optional[int] = None,
  1180	    target_price_override: Optional[float] = None,
  1181	    stop_loss_override: Optional[float] = None,
  1182	    *,
  1183	    has_position: Optional[bool] = None,
  1184	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  1185	    """Resolve the final structured fields once for both SSE payloads and DB writes."""
  1186	    market_report = sentiment_report = news_report = None
  1187	    fundamentals_report = macro_report = smart_money_report = volume_price_report = game_theory_report = None
  1188	    investment_plan = trader_investment_plan = None
  1189	    final_trade_decision = None
  1190	
  1191	    if result_data:
  1192	        market_report = result_data.get("market_report")
  1193	        sentiment_report = result_data.get("sentiment_report")
  1194	        news_report = result_data.get("news_report")
  1195	        fundamentals_report = result_data.get("fundamentals_report")
  1196	        macro_report = result_data.get("macro_report")
  1197	        smart_money_report = result_data.get("smart_money_report")
  1198	        volume_price_report = result_data.get("volume_price_report")
  1199	        game_theory_report = result_data.get("game_theory_report")
  1200	        investment_plan = result_data.get("investment_plan")
  1201	        trader_investment_plan = result_data.get("trader_investment_plan")
  1202	        final_trade_decision = result_data.get("final_trade_decision")
  1203	
  1204	    trusted_diagnostics_offset: Optional[int] = None
  1205	    if isinstance(result_data, dict):
  1206	        metadata = result_data.get("metadata")
  1207	        if isinstance(metadata, dict) and "system_diagnostics_offset" in metadata:
  1208	            trusted_diagnostics_offset = metadata.get("system_diagnostics_offset")
  1209	    model_authored_decision = _model_authored_report_text(
  1210	        final_trade_decision,
  1211	        trusted_diagnostics_offset,
  1212	    )
  1213	
  1214	    verdict = _extract_verdict(final_trade_decision)
  1215	    direction = verdict["direction"] if verdict else None
  1216	
  1217	    confidence = confidence_override if confidence_override is not None else _extract_confidence_regex(final_trade_decision)
  1218	
  1219	    price_sections = (
  1220	        final_trade_decision,
  1221	        trader_investment_plan,
  1222	        investment_plan,
  1223	        volume_price_report,
  1224	        market_report,
  1225	    )
  1226	    target_price = (
  1227	        target_price_override
  1228	        if target_price_override is not None
  1229	        else _extract_price_from_sections(price_sections, "target")
  1230	    )
  1231	
  1232	    stop_loss_price = (
  1233	        stop_loss_override
  1234	        if stop_loss_override is not None
  1235	        else _extract_price_from_sections(price_sections, "stop_loss")
  1236	    )
  1237	    if final_trade_decision and re.search(
  1238	        r"待定|不适用|取消|撤销|未设置|未给出|无法确定|暂无|"
  1239	        r"不(?:再)?(?:设置|设定|设|采用|使用|执行)|(?:^|[：:，,；;。\s])无(?:$|[，,；;。\s])|"
  1240	        r"not applicable|cancelled|canceled|withdrawn|unset|not set|\bTBD\b|\bN/?A\b|\bnone\b",
  1241	        model_authored_decision,
  1242	        re.IGNORECASE,
  1243	    ):
  1244	        # The final decision is authoritative. An explicit withdrawal must not
  1245	        # leak an older trader/manager stop into API fields or the UI. Preserve
  1246	        # the legacy dash-only convention, which denotes an omitted display
  1247	        # value rather than an explicit cancellation of an upstream risk level.
  1248	        from tradingagents.agents.utils.trade_setup import _field_explicitly_invalidated
  1249	
  1250	        if _field_explicitly_invalidated(
  1251	            model_authored_decision,
  1252	            (
  1253	                "止损价", "止损位", "初始止损", "硬性止损", "止损红线",
  1254	                "止损线", "止损", "失效价", "失效位", "防守位",
  1255	                "Stop-loss price", "Stop loss price", "Stop-loss", "Stop loss",
  1256	            ),
  1257	        ):
  1258	            stop_loss_price = None
  1259	
  1260	    research_direction = str(result_data.get("research_direction") or "") if result_data else ""

--- project overview ---
# 项目说明

## 项目定位

TradingAgents-AShare 是一个面向 A 股投资者的多智能体投研系统，核心理念：

- **模拟真实投研机构**：14 个专业角色分工协作
- **多空辩论机制**：多头研究员 vs 空头研究员，研究总监裁决
- **多模型厂商支持**：OpenAI / Anthropic / Google / 智谱 / DeepSeek / 小米等
- **意图驱动交互**：自然语言输入，自动识别标的和周期
- **持仓追踪**：导入持仓，自动跟踪，连续分析

## 当前产品事实（2026-07-10）

项目已从单一报告生成器扩展为分层投研工作台，但产品主线应保持收敛：

```text
Tree Work 既有研报/半年报知识页（历史上游）
  -> ZCode 知识库接入、研究评分与快照发布
  -> 来源与事实审计
  -> 昊天左侧候选
  -> TradeFlow 少量候选池
  -> 轻量 TA / 人工确认的完整 TA
  -> 观察仓、盘中观察、盘后复盘
```

TradeFlow 的 `TECH_TRADE` 与 `POLICY_AMBUSH/POLICY_CONFIRM` 必须分池展示，不能把短线技术机会当作中长期昊天候选。Fast Radar、Light Research、Full TA 的运行层级也必须保持清晰：完整 TA 只能人工确认触发。

现有 Tree Work 知识页只作为可追溯的历史上游与背景/观点证据；后续知识库接入、`research-scorer` 和正式快照发布统一由 ZCode 负责。公告、原始财报和行情数据仍是事实与动作门禁的优先来源。所有知识命中都必须带来源路径、报告期或更新时间、来源等级和新鲜度。

## 核心架构

### 1. 分析师团队（7 个）

| 角色 | 职责 | 数据源 |
|------|------|--------|
| 基本面分析师 | 财务指标、估值分析 | Tushare Pro（结构化主源）/AKShare/BaoStock |
| 情绪分析师 | 社交媒体情绪、舆情 | AKShare |
| 新闻分析师 | 新闻事件、政策影响 | AKShare |
| 技术分析师 | K线形态、技术指标 | AKShare/BaoStock |
| 宏观分析师 | 宏观经济、利率环境 | AKShare |
| 主力资金分析师 | 大单流向、主力动向 | Tushare Pro（个股资金主源）/AKShare |
| 量价分析师 | 成交量、量价关系 | AKShare/BaoStock |

### 2. 研究员团队

- **多头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看多 Claim
- **空头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看空 Claim
- **研究总监**：裁决多空辩论，形成投资计划

### 3. 风控团队

- **风控经理**：评估风险、设置止损止盈
- **合规检查**：确保建议符合投资规范

### 4. 交易员

- 根据研究计划和风控要求，输出最终交易建议

### 5. 编排层

- **LangGraph**：状态图编排，控制 Agent 间信息流转
- **辩论机制**：多轮辩论，每轮有 Claim → Counter-Claim → 裁决

## 数据流

```
用户输入（自然语言）
  ↓
意图识别 → 标的 + 周期
  ↓
数据获取（7 路并行）
  ↓
分析师团队（7 个并行分析）
  ↓
研究员辩论（多轮）
  ↓
风控评估
  ↓
交易建议输出
  ↓
研报存储 + 通知推送
```

## 模型配置

系统支持多层模型配置：

| 配置项 | 说明 | 默认值 |
|--------|------|--------|
| `deep_think_llm` | 深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `quick_think_llm` | 快速思考模型 | gpt-4o-mini |
| `mid_think_llm` | 中等思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `ultra_think_llm` | 超深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `backend_url` | API 端点 | https://api.openai.com/v1 |

支持通过环境变量或前端设置页切换模型厂商。

## API 接口

后端 FastAPI 提供以下主要接口：

- `POST /api/analyze` — 启动分析任务
- `GET /api/analysis/{id}` — 获取分析结果
- `GET /api/holdings` — 获取持仓
- `POST /api/watchlist` — 管理自选股
- `GET /api/reports` — 历史研报
- `POST /api/scheduler` — 定时任务管理

## 前端功能

- 意图驱动的自然语言交互
- 辩论对战可视化（多空对抗 Drawer）
- 持仓追踪与跟踪看板
- 结构化研报管理
- 定时分析配置
- 模型厂商设置

# 架构决策记录

> 记录项目中的关键架构决策，方便后续回溯"为什么这样做"。

---

## ADR-001: 采用 LangGraph 编排多智能体
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要 14 个 Agent 按特定流程协作，有循环（辩论）和条件分支
- **决策**：使用 LangGraph 状态图编排，而非简单的链式调用
- **原因**：LangGraph 原生支持循环、条件路由、状态管理，适合辩论机制
- **影响**：所有 Agent 间通信通过 Graph State 传递，新增 Agent 需要注册到图中

## ADR-002: 多模型厂商适配层
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：不同 Agent 对模型能力需求不同，用户可能有不同的模型偏好
- **决策**：通过 `llm_clients/` 统一适配，支持通过配置切换厂商和模型
- **原因**：避免硬编码单一厂商，降低用户使用门槛
- **影响**：新增模型厂商需要在 `llm_clients/` 中添加适配器

## ADR-003: SQLite 作为持久化方案
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要存储研报、自选股、定时任务等结构化数据
- **决策**：使用 SQLite，单文件部署，无需额外数据库服务
- **原因**：本地部署场景，轻量级，零运维
- **影响**：并发写入有限，定时任务和分析任务需要串行化写入

## ADR-004: OpenClaw 作为项目协作调度层
- **日期**：2026-05-09
- **背景**：需要 OpenClaw、OpenCode、OpenCode TUI 三者协作处理同一项目
- **决策**：OpenClaw 负责任务判断和调度，不直接做代码修改；OpenCode 负责代码执行；OpenCode TUI 负责审查
- **原因**：OpenClaw 有完整的 agent 调度能力（exec / sessions_spawn / cron），适合做编排层
- **影响**：所有代码修改任务必须经过 OpenClaw 判断复杂度后决定执行路径

## ADR-005: Git 仓库 + docs/ 作为项目事实源
- **日期**：2026-05-09
- **背景**：多个工具需要读取同一套项目上下文
- **决策**：不使用任何 agent 的独立记忆系统保存项目状态，所有关键信息写入 Git 仓库的 docs/ 目录
- **原因**：避免信息分散、版本冲突、单点失效
- **影响**：每次任务开始前必须读取 AGENTS.md + docs/ 下的上下文文件

## ADR-006: 2026-07-10 将研报/半年报/昊天闭环设为唯一产品主线
- **日期**：2026-07-10
- **背景**：系统已有 TradeFlow、跟踪看板、知识库和自动开发等大量工程能力，但真实候选中仍以技术池为主，观察仓和盘后 Review 的生产记录不足，半年报事实 Provider 尚未完成。
- **决策**：优先完成 HY-002/HY-003/KB-015 及其后续事实审计，再做昊天真实候选和交易日回放；PLAYBOOK-002~006 暂停，除非直接阻塞主线。
- **原因**：用户核心目标是从政策、产业和研报中找到少量高质量中长期候选，而不是继续扩大功能数量。先验证选股和证据闭环，才能判断后续仓位规则是否值得实现。
- **影响**：自动领取顺序以 `docs/TASKS.md` 的“当前执行队列”为准；fixture 验收和真实运行验收必须分开记录；新功能必须说明是否直接服务主线。

## ADR-007: 研究评分采用四卡分层所有权，不生成混合总分
- **日期**：2026-07-13
- **背景**：现有 Tree Work 知识库、TA readiness、TradeFlow 候选分、账户风控和 PLAYBOOK 已分别存在评分或阶段字段。若各层都计算完整总分和动作，会产生重复计分、阈值冲突，以及“好公司被误判为现在可以买”的风险。
- **决策**：`research-scorer` 的代码、规则版本和正式快照写入归 ZCode 知识库接入项目所有；ZCode 发布的知识快照只拥有慢变量 `research_evidence_confidence` 与 `thesis_quality`。现有 Tree Work 页面继续作为上游知识输入，不要求历史改名。TradingAgents-AShare 不复刻评分公式，只读取版本化快照；TradeFlow 复用现有动态因子生成 `entry_timing`，账户上下文生成 `portfolio_fit`，TA 在四卡之后统一执行硬性否决并映射 PLAYBOOK 七阶段。禁止合成单一股票总分，禁止知识快照携带或覆盖交易动作。
- **原因**：证据可信度、公司逻辑、实时买点和账户资格回答的是四个不同问题；分层后才能做到来源可追溯、动态变化可解释，并让高研究分始终受行情、账户和风控约束。
- **影响**：知识库侧任务单独记录在 `docs/zcode_research_scorer_handoff.md`；本仓库新增 SCORE-001~006 消费与裁决任务线。`execution_action` 与 Buy/Risk Level 继续作为底层安全契约，`playbook_stage` 只提供更细语义。SCORE-005 可以实现不含仓位数量的阶段判定，但不解锁 PLAYBOOK-002/003 的真实三笔法和仓位拆分规则。SCORE-006 必须等真实 ZCode 快照人工确认后释放，fixture 不得冒充跨项目验收。

---

## 决策模板（后续新增时复制）

```
## ADR-NNN: [决策标题]
- **日期**：YYYY-MM-DD
- **背景**：[为什么需要做这个决策]
- **决策**：[做了什么决定]
- **原因**：[为什么选这个方案]
- **影响**：[对项目的影响]
```

# B-002-R2 Implementation Result

- Task: B-002-R2 - OpenClaw 回调 ORM 字段与 readiness 真源修复（P1）
- Implemented at: 2026-08-17
- Status: IMPLEMENTED (awaiting Codex review / outer-loop state transition)

## Files changed

- `scheduler/main.py` — `_load_report_payload` only reads real `ReportDB`
  columns; removed `horizon` / `analysis_summary` / `opinion` reads
  (AttributeError source) and the always-None `readiness_score` getattr.
- `api/services/openclaw_callback_service.py` — readiness now parsed from the
  persisted `[C-008]` block in `final_trade_decision` (structured dict kept as
  forward-compatible priority); `readiness_score` always present in payload
  with explicit `available` / `not_available` status; schema version 1.1.0.
  [R2 fix] Parsing is restricted to the trusted system tail via persisted
  `metadata.system_diagnostics_offset`; a model-authored `[C-008]` mention can
  no longer be published as `available`.
- `tests/test_b002_openclaw_callback.py` — updated contract assertions, added
  `TestReadinessScoreSources` (8 tests), `TestSchedulerCallbackOrmAlignment`
  (3 tests: schema-key alignment vs `sqlalchemy.inspect(ReportDB).columns`,
  enabled-callback full-payload integration, missing-report-row path), and
  `TestReadinessTrustedOffset` (10 tests for the trusted-tail fix).
- `docs/DEVLOG.md` — entry added.

## Key logic

- P1: `ReportDB` has no `horizon/analysis_summary/opinion/readiness_score`
  columns (verified via AttributeError probe on a real instance). The loader
  now maps only real columns with direct attribute access; `horizon` continues
  to flow from the scheduled task argument.
- P2: real persisted readiness source = `[C-008] 执行就绪度评分` text block
  appended by risk_manager into `final_trade_decision` (ReportDB column and
  result_data key). [R2 fix] The parser first reads the persisted
  `result_data.metadata.system_diagnostics_offset` (the same trusted field
  report_service and the frontend already consume) and restricts parsing to
  that system-appended tail: a `[C-008]` mention in the model-authored body —
  even one carrying completeness/confidence values — is never published. An
  offset that is present but invalid (non-int / out of range) yields
  `not_available`. Legacy reports without the metadata fall back to the LAST
  `[C-008]` heading (the system block is appended after model text); a
  malformed last block does not leak earlier model-authored values.
  Completeness outside 0-100 is dropped; text without the marker is not
  trusted.
- Contract: `payload["readiness_score"]` is always present —
  `{"status": "available", "data_completeness": int|None, "confidence": str|None,
  "source": "readiness_score"|"final_trade_decision"}` or
  `{"status": "not_available"}`. No empty-value faking.

--- project overview ---
# 项目说明

## 项目定位

TradingAgents-AShare 是一个面向 A 股投资者的多智能体投研系统，核心理念：

- **模拟真实投研机构**：14 个专业角色分工协作
- **多空辩论机制**：多头研究员 vs 空头研究员，研究总监裁决
- **多模型厂商支持**：OpenAI / Anthropic / Google / 智谱 / DeepSeek / 小米等
- **意图驱动交互**：自然语言输入，自动识别标的和周期
- **持仓追踪**：导入持仓，自动跟踪，连续分析

## 当前产品事实（2026-07-10）

项目已从单一报告生成器扩展为分层投研工作台，但产品主线应保持收敛：

```text
Tree Work 既有研报/半年报知识页（历史上游）
  -> ZCode 知识库接入、研究评分与快照发布
  -> 来源与事实审计
  -> 昊天左侧候选
  -> TradeFlow 少量候选池
  -> 轻量 TA / 人工确认的完整 TA
  -> 观察仓、盘中观察、盘后复盘
```

TradeFlow 的 `TECH_TRADE` 与 `POLICY_AMBUSH/POLICY_CONFIRM` 必须分池展示，不能把短线技术机会当作中长期昊天候选。Fast Radar、Light Research、Full TA 的运行层级也必须保持清晰：完整 TA 只能人工确认触发。

现有 Tree Work 知识页只作为可追溯的历史上游与背景/观点证据；后续知识库接入、`research-scorer` 和正式快照发布统一由 ZCode 负责。公告、原始财报和行情数据仍是事实与动作门禁的优先来源。所有知识命中都必须带来源路径、报告期或更新时间、来源等级和新鲜度。

## 核心架构

### 1. 分析师团队（7 个）

| 角色 | 职责 | 数据源 |
|------|------|--------|
| 基本面分析师 | 财务指标、估值分析 | Tushare Pro（结构化主源）/AKShare/BaoStock |
| 情绪分析师 | 社交媒体情绪、舆情 | AKShare |
| 新闻分析师 | 新闻事件、政策影响 | AKShare |
| 技术分析师 | K线形态、技术指标 | AKShare/BaoStock |
| 宏观分析师 | 宏观经济、利率环境 | AKShare |
| 主力资金分析师 | 大单流向、主力动向 | Tushare Pro（个股资金主源）/AKShare |
| 量价分析师 | 成交量、量价关系 | AKShare/BaoStock |

### 2. 研究员团队

- **多头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看多 Claim
- **空头研究员**：综合分析师结论，构建看空 Claim
- **研究总监**：裁决多空辩论，形成投资计划

### 3. 风控团队

- **风控经理**：评估风险、设置止损止盈
- **合规检查**：确保建议符合投资规范

### 4. 交易员

- 根据研究计划和风控要求，输出最终交易建议

### 5. 编排层

- **LangGraph**：状态图编排，控制 Agent 间信息流转
- **辩论机制**：多轮辩论，每轮有 Claim → Counter-Claim → 裁决

## 数据流

```
用户输入（自然语言）
  ↓
意图识别 → 标的 + 周期
  ↓
数据获取（7 路并行）
  ↓
分析师团队（7 个并行分析）
  ↓
研究员辩论（多轮）
  ↓
风控评估
  ↓
交易建议输出
  ↓
研报存储 + 通知推送
```

## 模型配置

系统支持多层模型配置：

| 配置项 | 说明 | 默认值 |
|--------|------|--------|
| `deep_think_llm` | 深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `quick_think_llm` | 快速思考模型 | gpt-4o-mini |
| `mid_think_llm` | 中等思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `ultra_think_llm` | 超深度思考模型 | gpt-4o |
| `backend_url` | API 端点 | https://api.openai.com/v1 |

支持通过环境变量或前端设置页切换模型厂商。

## API 接口

后端 FastAPI 提供以下主要接口：

- `POST /api/analyze` — 启动分析任务
- `GET /api/analysis/{id}` — 获取分析结果
- `GET /api/holdings` — 获取持仓
- `POST /api/watchlist` — 管理自选股
- `GET /api/reports` — 历史研报
- `POST /api/scheduler` — 定时任务管理

## 前端功能

- 意图驱动的自然语言交互
- 辩论对战可视化（多空对抗 Drawer）
- 持仓追踪与跟踪看板
- 结构化研报管理
- 定时分析配置
- 模型厂商设置
--- decisions recent ---
# 架构决策记录

> 记录项目中的关键架构决策，方便后续回溯"为什么这样做"。

---

## ADR-001: 采用 LangGraph 编排多智能体
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要 14 个 Agent 按特定流程协作，有循环（辩论）和条件分支
- **决策**：使用 LangGraph 状态图编排，而非简单的链式调用
- **原因**：LangGraph 原生支持循环、条件路由、状态管理，适合辩论机制
- **影响**：所有 Agent 间通信通过 Graph State 传递，新增 Agent 需要注册到图中

## ADR-002: 多模型厂商适配层
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：不同 Agent 对模型能力需求不同，用户可能有不同的模型偏好
- **决策**：通过 `llm_clients/` 统一适配，支持通过配置切换厂商和模型
- **原因**：避免硬编码单一厂商，降低用户使用门槛
- **影响**：新增模型厂商需要在 `llm_clients/` 中添加适配器

## ADR-003: SQLite 作为持久化方案
- **日期**：项目初始化
- **背景**：需要存储研报、自选股、定时任务等结构化数据

```
--- FINAL CODEX REVIEW ---
The ORM mapping fix works and the focused tests pass, but readiness parsing can still publish model-authored values instead of the authoritative system block. The run archive also references the wrong test artifact.

  When the model-generated portion of `final_trade_decision` mentions `[C-008]` before the system-appended block, these searches return the model's completeness and confidence rather than the authoritative values appended later. Since production already persists `metadata.system_diagnostics_offset`, restrict parsing to that trusted tail—or at least select the last complete C-008 heading—so fabricated readiness is not published as `available`.
```

     compact = " ".join(str(text).split()).strip()

+# [B-002-R2] readiness 真源：risk_manager 把 [C-008] 执行就绪度评分块追加到
+# final_trade_decision 文本并随 ReportDB 持久化；结构化 dict 无生产写入方，
+# 仅作为前向兼容输入。文本块是当前唯一真实持久化来源。
+_C008_MARKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\[C-008\]")
+_READINESS_COMPLETENESS_PATTERN = re.compile(r"数据完整度[：:]\s*(\d{1,3})\s*%")
+_READINESS_CONFIDENCE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"置信度[：:]\s*([高中低])")
+
+
+def _parse_readiness_from_text(text: Any) -> dict | None:
+    """Parse the persisted [C-008] readiness block from report text.
+
+    Returns ``{"data_completeness": int, "confidence": str}`` (either key may
+    be present alone) or None when the block or its values are absent/invalid.
+    """
+    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
+        return None
+    marker = _C008_MARKER_PATTERN.search(text)
+    if not marker:
+        return None
+    section = text[marker.end():]
+    parsed: dict[str, Any] = {}
+    completeness = _READINESS_COMPLETENESS_PATTERN.search(section)
+    if completeness:
+        value = int(completeness.group(1))
+        if 0 <= value <= 100:
+            parsed["data_completeness"] = value
+    confidence = _READINESS_CONFIDENCE_PATTERN.search(section)
+    if confidence:
+        parsed["confidence"] = confidence.group(1)
+    return parsed or None
+
+
 def is_openclaw_callback_enabled() -> bool:
     """Check if OpenClaw callback is enabled and URL is configured."""
     url = os.getenv("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_URL", "").strip()
     enabled = _env_bool("OPENCLAW_CALLBACK_ENABLED", False)

     1	# B-002-R2 Implementation Result
     2	
     3	- Task: B-002-R2 - OpenClaw 回调 ORM 字段与 readiness 真源修复（P1）

     5	- Status: IMPLEMENTED (awaiting Codex review / outer-loop state transition)
     6	
     7	## Files changed
     8	
     9	- `scheduler/main.py` — `_load_report_payload` only reads real `ReportDB`
    10	  columns; removed `horizon` / `analysis_summary` / `opinion` reads
    11	  (AttributeError source) and the always-None `readiness_score` getattr.
    12	- `api/services/openclaw_callback_service.py` — readiness now parsed from the
    13	  persisted `[C-008]` block in `final_trade_decision` (structured dict kept as
    14	  forward-compatible priority); `readiness_score` always present in payload
    15	  with explicit `available` / `not_available` status; schema version 1.1.0.
    16	  [R2 fix] Parsing is restricted to the trusted system tail via persisted
    17	  `metadata.system_diagnostics_offset`; a model-authored `[C-008]` mention can
    18	  no longer be published as `available`.
    19	- `tests/test_b002_openclaw_callback.py` — updated contract assertions, added
    20	  `TestReadinessScoreSources` (8 tests), `TestSchedulerCallbackOrmAlignment`
    21	  (3 tests: schema-key alignment vs `sqlalchemy.inspect(ReportDB).columns`,
    22	  enabled-callback full-payload integration, missing-report-row path), and
    23	  `TestReadinessTrustedOffset` (10 tests for the trusted-tail fix).
    24	- `docs/DEVLOG.md` — entry added.
    25	
    26	## Key logic
    27	
    28	- P1: `ReportDB` has no `horizon/analysis_summary/opinion/readiness_score`
    29	  columns (verified via AttributeError probe on a real instance). The loader
    30	  now maps only real columns with direct attribute access; `horizon` continues
    31	  to flow from the scheduled task argument.
    32	- P2: real persisted readiness source = `[C-008] 执行就绪度评分` text block
    33	  appended by risk_manager into `final_trade_decision` (ReportDB column and
    34	  result_data key). [R2 fix] The parser first reads the persisted
    35	  `result_data.metadata.system_diagnostics_offset` (the same trusted field
    36	  report_service and the frontend already consume) and restricts parsing to
    37	  that system-appended tail: a `[C-008]` mention in the model-authored body —
    38	  even one carrying completeness/confidence values — is never published. An
    39	  offset that is present but invalid (non-int / out of range) yields
    40	  `not_available`. Legacy reports without the metadata fall back to the LAST
    41	  `[C-008]` heading (the system block is appended after model text); a
    42	  malformed last block does not leak earlier model-authored values.
    43	  Completeness outside 0-100 is dropped; text without the marker is not
    44	  trusted.
    45	- Contract: `payload["readiness_score"]` is always present —
    46	  `{"status": "available", "data_completeness": int|None, "confidence": str|None,
    47	  "source": "readiness_score"|"final_trade_decision"}` or
    48	  `{"status": "not_available"}`. No empty-value faking.
    49	
    50	## Test evidence

   162	        or any(not is_entry_only_blocker(blocker) for blocker in quality_blockers)
   163	    ):
   164	        action = "WAIT/等待人工复核"
   165	    elif risk_level == 3:
   166	        action = "触发止损"
   167	    elif risk_level == 2:
   168	        action = "条件减仓"
   169	    elif trade_quality_check.get("exit_intent"):
   170	        # An exit recommendation without independently confirmed sell-side risk
   171	        # is a conflict, not a HOLD. Keep it fail-closed for human review.
   172	        action = "WAIT/等待人工复核"
   173	    else:
   174	        action = (
   175	            "WAIT/等待人工复核"
   176	            if trade_quality_check.get("do_not_trade_if")
   177	            else "HOLD/持有"
   178	        )
   179	    gate_status = "通过" if gate.get("passed", True) else "未通过"
   180	    return (
   181	        "### 系统执行结论\n"
   182	        f"- 系统动作：{action}\n"
   183	        f"- 强动作门禁：{gate_status}\n"
   184	        f"- Buy Level：{buy_level}\n"
   185	        f"- Risk Level：{risk_level}"
   186	    )
   187	
   188	
   189	def _completed_bar_can_supply_execution_price(
   190	    completed_bar,
   191	    trade_date: str,
   192	    *,
   193	    symbol: str = "",
   194	    now: datetime | None = None,
   195	) -> bool:
   196	    """Allow a close fallback only when that instrument's session is final."""
   197	    if not completed_bar or not trade_date:
   198	        return False
   199	    analysis_date = str(trade_date)[:10]
   200	    bar_date = str(completed_bar.get("date") or "")[:10]
   201	    if not analysis_date or not bar_date:
   202	        return False
   203	    market_context = build_market_context(symbol, analysis_date, now=now)
   204	    if market_context.get("analysis_mode") == "historical":
   205	        return bar_date == analysis_date
   206	    if market_context.get("analysis_mode") == "closed":
   207	        return False
   208	    return bar_date == analysis_date and is_daily_bar_final(
   209	        symbol,
   210	        analysis_date,
   420	        # that depend on the complete response, so emitting here could briefly
   421	        # expose an action that the final decision correctly forbids.
   422	        short_stream_filter = ShortSellingStreamFilter(can_short=can_short)
   423	
   424	        async for chunk in llm.astream(prompt):
   425	            content = chunk.content if hasattr(chunk, "content") else str(chunk)
   426	            safe_token = short_stream_filter.feed(content)
   427	            full_content += safe_token
   428	
   429	        # [C-003-R1] Flush the buffered tail (sanitized) into full_content.
   430	        flushed_tail = short_stream_filter.finalize()
   431	        if flushed_tail:
   432	            full_content += flushed_tail
   433	        if short_stream_filter.changes:
   434	            _logger.warning(
   435	                "[C-003-R1] risk short_stream_filter applied: %s",
   436	                short_stream_filter.changes,
   437	            )
   438	
   439	        judge_result = extract_risk_judge_result(full_content)
   440	        cleaned_response = judge_result["cleaned_response"]
   441	        verdict = judge_result["verdict"]
   442	        hard_constraints = judge_result["hard_constraints"]
   443	        soft_constraints = judge_result["soft_constraints"]
   444	        execution_preconditions = judge_result["execution_preconditions"]
   445	        de_risk_triggers = judge_result["de_risk_triggers"]
   446	        revision_reason = judge_result["revision_reason"]
   447	        trade_quality_check = build_trade_quality_check(
   448	            investment_plan=state.get("investment_plan", ""),
   449	            trader_plan=trader_plan,
   450	            final_decision=cleaned_response,
   451	            user_context=state.get("user_context", {}),
   452	            position_context=state.get("position_context"),
   453	        )
   454	        final_response = (
   455	            cleaned_response
   456	            + "\n\n"
   457	            + _SYSTEM_DIAGNOSTICS_START
   458	            + "\n"
   459	            + format_trade_quality_check(trade_quality_check)
   460	        )
   461	
   462	        # [C-001] position_validation_gate — 校验输出动作
   463	        from tradingagents.agents.utils.position_validation_gate import (
   464	            validate_position_actions,
   465	            format_position_validation_warning,
   466	        )
   467	        gate_result = validate_position_actions(final_response, user_context)
   468	        if not gate_result["passed"]:
   469	            final_response += format_position_validation_warning(gate_result)
   470	
   471	        # [C-005] delta_check — 检测结论是否翻转
   472	        delta_info = check_delta(stock_code, final_response, ["risk_manager"])
   473	        if delta_info is not None:
   474	            final_response += format_delta_warning(delta_info)
   475	            _logger.warning("[C-005] delta_check: %s 结论翻转, possible_noise=%s", stock_code, delta_info["possible_noise"])

    60	def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    61	    """Get database session (for FastAPI Depends)."""
    62	    db = SessionLocal()
    63	    try:
    64	        yield db
    65	    finally:
    66	        db.close()
    67	
    68	
    69	class get_db_ctx:
    70	    """Context manager for manual DB session usage.
    71	
    72	    Usage:
    73	        with get_db_ctx() as db:
    74	            db.query(...)
    75	    """
    76	
    77	    def __init__(self) -> None:
    78	        self.db: Session | None = None
    79	
    80	    def __enter__(self) -> Session:
    81	        self.db = SessionLocal()
    82	        return self.db
    83	
    84	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb) -> None:
    85	        if self.db is not None:
    86	            if exc_type is not None:
    87	                self.db.rollback()
    88	            self.db.close()
    89

codex
The ORM mapping fix is sound, but the new readiness contract can still trust an explicitly invalid offset and can label incomplete or invalid scores as available. These issues undermine the callback's claimed fail-closed readiness semantics.

Full review comments:

- [P2] Preserve present-null offsets as invalid — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/api/services/openclaw_callback_service.py:100-102
  When metadata explicitly contains `system_diagnostics_offset: null`, this helper returns the same `None` used for an absent key, so parsing falls back to the legacy last-`[C-008]` heuristic. A malformed report whose model-authored text ends with such a block can therefore publish fabricated readiness despite the documented rule that present-but-invalid offsets fail closed; distinguish true absence from an explicit null value.

- [P2] Require a complete readiness score before marking it available — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/api/services/openclaw_callback_service.py:281-285
  When a `[C-008]` block is truncated or has an out-of-range completeness value, or a structured snapshot contains partial or wrongly typed values, `readiness_values` remains non-null and this emits `status: available` with null or invalid fields. That contradicts the v1.1 contract requiring an integer completeness and valid confidence and can make consumers trust a malformed score; validate both fields before assigning `available`, otherwise return `not_available`.
The ORM mapping fix is sound, but the new readiness contract can still trust an explicitly invalid offset and can label incomplete or invalid scores as available. These issues undermine the callback's claimed fail-closed readiness semantics.
